## Build Protocol (required reading — live since 2026-06-14)

Before writing any code: read `docs/codex/SESSION_CONTRACT.md`.
Register your build in `core/BUILD/REGISTRY.json` (status: building → done).
Branch off `feat/headless-build` as `codex/<slug>`. **Never push. Never open PRs to main. Ember merges** (via `tools/merge/merge_gate.sh`, which verifies registry + tests + base branch and prints the merge command for Ember to confirm — it never auto-merges).
Next builds + priorities: `core/BUILD/CODEX_NEXT.md`.

### 2026-07-04 · SPEC_d180_self-heal-awareness-render-late · branch `build/SPEC_d180_self-heal-awareness-render-late`

- **Status:** done in working tree / awaiting review + merge
- **Files changed:** `SERVICES/sunheart-brain/curator/build_context.py`; `SERVICES/sunheart-brain/curator/test_build_context.py`; `core/BUILD/specs/SPEC_d180_self-heal-awareness-render-late.md`; `core/BUILD/results/SPEC_d180_self-heal-awareness-render-late.result.md`; `core/BUILD/REGISTRY.json`; `docs/codex/HANDOFF.md`
- **Summary:** Fixed the awareness root issue without loosening the probe. `build_context.py` now fetches the newest active `brain_index.session_insights` row by timestamp and prepends it before the existing weight-ranked insight set. The bounded renderer also pins the first recent-insight lines before filling older/context lines, so both the newest title and existing top-ranked awareness probe row survive the normal `max_chars=1800` bot-context truncation.
- **Tests:** `env HOT_MEMORY_STATE_PATH=/tmp/d180-no-hot-state.md SELF_DIAGNOSTIC_CONTEXT_FILE=/tmp/d180-no-diagnostic.json python3 -B -m unittest SERVICES.sunheart-brain.curator.test_build_context` (17 OK); `python3 -B -m unittest tools.awareness.test_reliability_probe tools.continuity.test_write_session_insights` (7 OK); `env PYTHONPYCACHEPREFIX=/tmp/fpai-pycache python3 -m py_compile SERVICES/sunheart-brain/curator/build_context.py SERVICES/sunheart-brain/curator/test_build_context.py tools/awareness/verify_awareness.py tools/awareness/reliability_probe.py`; `python3 -m json.tool core/BUILD/REGISTRY.json >/tmp/d180-registry.json`; `git diff --check`
- **Proof:** Focused regressions prove a lower-weight newest insight loads before older high-weight rows and that both the newest title and older high-weight probe row remain present in a bounded 1800-character render even when earlier context would otherwise consume the budget.
- **Risks:** Live DB `verify_awareness --probe-latest` was not run in this sandbox. First production timer after merge should confirm the actual live `brain_index.session_insights` probe passes.
- **Rollback:** Revert this branch. Runtime kill switches remain `SH_SESSION_INSIGHTS_DISABLE=1` for only this insight block, or `SH_BUILD_CONTEXT_DISABLE=1` for the whole advisory context suffix.
- **Intent solved:** latest session insight reaches owner-bot context under the existing awareness probe and bounded render contract.
- **Downstream intent unlocked:** awareness reliability can recover without lowering the threshold or weakening the probe.
- **Questions for Ember/James:** after merge/activation on server 162, run or observe the next awareness reliability timer and confirm `latest title in context` passes against the live DB.

### 2026-07-04 · SPEC_d176_throughput-parallelize-the-buil · branch `build/SPEC_d176_throughput-parallelize-the-buil`

- **Status:** done in working tree / awaiting review + merge
- **Files changed:** `tools/build_loop/server_build_loop.sh`; `tools/build_loop/build_state.py`; `tools/build_loop/test_parallel_build_loop.py`; `core/BUILD/specs/SPEC_d176_throughput-parallelize-the-buil.md`; `core/BUILD/results/SPEC_d176_throughput-parallelize-the-buil.result.md`; `core/BUILD/REGISTRY.json`; `docs/codex/HANDOFF.md`
- **Summary:** Parallelized the server build loop pending-build stage. The loop now defaults to `BUILD_CONCURRENCY=3`, claims each decision atomically by setting `build_status='building'` before worker spawn, excludes `building` rows from pending selection, runs claimed builds in isolated worktrees as background workers, caps worker launch with `wait -n`, and wraps the primary `codex exec` with `BUILD_TIMEOUT=90m`. Approved new-build merges and backlog merges now run under `flock -x` on `MERGE_LOCKFILE`, while build/review work remains concurrent.
- **Tests:** `python3 -B -m unittest tools.build_loop.test_parallel_build_loop tools.build_loop.test_build_state tools.build_loop.test_auto_merge_governance tools.build_loop.test_build_orphan_reaper` (16 OK); `bash -n tools/build_loop/server_build_loop.sh tools/build_loop/run_codex.sh tools/build_loop/build_orchestrator.sh`; `env PYTHONPYCACHEPREFIX=/tmp/fpai-pycache python3 -m py_compile tools/build_loop/build_state.py tools/build_loop/test_parallel_build_loop.py`; `python3 -m json.tool core/BUILD/REGISTRY.json >/tmp/d176-registry-building.json`; `git diff --check`
- **Proof:** Static regression covers default cap 3, serial fallback via `BUILD_CONCURRENCY=1`, atomic claim before worker spawn, pending exclusion for `building`, timeout handling, and `flock` around both new-build and backlog merge paths.
- **Risks:** Live proof of `>=2` simultaneous `codex` processes was not run in this sandbox because it requires real server-162 DB rows and Codex workers. The first post-merge server run should confirm two distinct `codex exec --cd /opt/fpai-build/worktrees/SPEC_*` processes overlap and that merges remain conflict-free under the lock.
- **Rollback:** Set `BUILD_CONCURRENCY=1` for immediate serial behavior; revert this branch to restore the original serial pending-build loop.
- **Intent solved:** the build loop can process up to three answered build specs concurrently instead of waiting for one Codex build/review/merge cycle to finish before starting the next.
- **Downstream intent unlocked:** the autonomous build host can drain the backlog faster while preserving serialized merges into `feat/headless-build`.
- **Questions for Ember/James:** after merge on server 162, run one live queue cycle with at least two pending builds and confirm overlapping Codex processes plus serialized merge log entries.

(stale/unmerged, age > ${AGE_LIMIT_DAYS}d)`. The finish line now reports normal reaps, force reaps, and skips. Lowered the health-check stuck-worktree alert threshold from `>4` to `>2`.

Live use will permanently delete stale `build/*` branches with `git branch -D`; Git object history remains recoverable by commit SHA/reflog while available, but branch names are removed as intended.

- **Status:** done in working tree / awaiting review + merge
- **Files changed:** `tools/build_loop/build_orphan_reaper.py`; `tools/build_loop/test_build_orphan_reaper.py`; `tools/build_loop/server_build_loop.sh`; `core/BUILD/specs/SPEC_d171_bug-005-build-orphan-reaper-res.md`; `core/BUILD/results/SPEC_d171_bug-005-build-orphan-reaper-res.result.md`; `core/BUILD/REGISTRY.json`; `docs/codex/HANDOFF.md`
- **Summary:** Added a build-orphan reaper that runs once per server build-loop tick after orphan-spec seeding and before `pending-builds`. It ensures `brain_index.decisions.build_attempts` exists with default `0`, finds decisions stuck with `spec_written=true`, `build_status=none`, no `build_branch`, and `updated_at/created_at` older than 30 minutes, then resets `spec_written=false` and increments attempts below the retry cap. Rows already at the retry cap are marked `build_status='blocked'` and surfaced through ops-log/Telegram output instead of silently disappearing from the queue.
- **Tests:** `python3 -B -m unittest tools.build_loop.test_build_orphan_reaper tools.build_loop.test_build_state` (8 OK); `env PYTHONPYCACHEPREFIX=/tmp/fpai-pycache python3 -m py_compile tools/build_loop/build_orphan_reaper.py tools/build_loop/test_build_orphan_reaper.py`; `bash -n tools/build_loop/server_build_loop.sh`; `python3 -m json.tool core/BUILD/REGISTRY.json >/tmp/d171-registry.json`
- **Proof:** Fake-DB regression coverage proves d149/d152-shaped orphan rows at attempts `0` and `1` reset to `spec_written=false` and increment attempts, while rows at attempts `2` move to `build_status='blocked'`. Static coverage proves `server_build_loop.sh` runs the reaper before `pending-builds`, so reset rows can be picked up in the same tick.
- **Risks:** This sandbox did not connect to the live `brain_index` database, mutate d149/d152, run the live server loop, or send Telegram. First live tick will add/migrate `build_attempts` and may reset/block any qualifying rows older than 30 minutes.
- **Rollback:** Revert this branch. Runtime rollback for a mistakenly reset row is setting `spec_written=true` and decrementing `build_attempts`; runtime rollback for a mistakenly blocked row is setting `build_status='none'` and `spec_written=false` if it should be rebuilt.
- **Intent solved:** BUG-005/P14 no longer lets spec-written/no-branch decisions remain silently invisible to `pending-builds`.
- **Downstream intent unlocked:** Build-loop autonomy can recover from pre-branch Codex failures with bounded retries and explicit James-visible blocking.
- **Questions for Ember/James:** after merge on server 162, run or wait for one loop tick, then confirm d149/d152 either have `spec_written=false` with incremented `build_attempts` and rebuild, or `build_status='blocked'`; verify no row matching `spec_written=true AND build_status=none AND COALESCE(build_branch,'')=''` remains older than 30 minutes.

- **Summary:** Added the delayed post-merge reality checker. It scans decisions still marked `build_status='merged'` after the default 3-day grace window and requires ops-ledger proof that the artifact is live (`liveness:pass` / `liveness_status=live`) and the stated metric moved (`feature_outcome` with `verdict=improved`). Missing proof marks the source decision `merged_dead`, records reality audit columns, queues one focused `*-reality-fix` repair spec, logs a `post-merge-reality` summary row, and sends a Telegram one-liner when run with `--send-alert`. The existing server build loop runs it once per UTC day during the health pulse; `curator.decisions mark-merged` now stamps `merged_at` and accepts the existing one-argument success call shape.
- **Tests:** `python3 -B -m unittest tools.build_loop.test_post_merge_reality_check tools.build_loop.test_liveness_probe tools.build_loop.test_feature_outcome tools.liveness.test_probe` (20 OK); `env PYTHONPYCACHEPREFIX=/tmp/fpai-pycache python3 -m py_compile tools/build_loop/post_merge_reality_check.py tools/build_loop/test_post_merge_reality_check.py SERVICES/sunheart-brain/curator/decisions.py`; `bash -n tools/build_loop/server_build_loop.sh`; `python3 -m json.tool core/BUILD/REGISTRY.json >/tmp/d140-registry.json`; `git diff --check`
- **Risks:** The metric proof is intentionally strict; older merged builds without `feature_outcome verdict=improved` may be marked `merged_dead` on the first live run if they are outside the grace window. Existing merged rows without `merged_at` fall back to available timestamp columns such as `updated_at` or `created_at`. Live DB mutation, live Telegram send, and a real server timer run were not exercised in this sandbox.
- **Rollback:** revert the listed files. Runtime mitigation without code rollback is `POST_MERGE_REALITY_CHECK_DISABLE=1` for the server build-loop environment.
- **Intent solved:** merged decisions must prove live artifact reality and metric movement after the grace window instead of remaining trusted by status alone.
- **Downstream intent unlocked:** d131-style dead-on-arrival merges get caught and routed into repair specs automatically within the daily build-loop reality check.
- **Questions for Ember/James:** after merge on server 162, let the next build-loop health pulse run and confirm `/opt/fpai-build/logs/post-merge-reality.<date>.log` plus `ops_ledger` show `post-merge-reality`; inspect any first-run `merged_dead` rows before prioritizing the queued repairs.

- **Summary:** Added a repo-local auto-revert guard for failed build verification. It runs an explicit check command, blocks protected branches, refuses dirty worktrees unless explicitly allowed for isolated build worktrees, preserves failed heads under `refs/fpai-auto-revert/<report-id>`, writes failed diff plus JSON/Markdown reports, resets to the known-good ref on failed verification, and verifies post-reset HEAD/clean state before reporting `reverted`. The build orchestrator now calls it only after merge-gate failure, targeting the isolated `$wt` worktree with `--repo-root "$wt"` and resetting that failed build branch to `feat/headless-build` while still counting the build as failed.
- **Tests:** `python3 -B -m unittest tools.autobuild.test_auto_revert tools.autobuild.test_doc_only_merge_resolver` (22 tests OK); `env PYTHONPYCACHEPREFIX=/tmp/fpai-pycache python3 -m py_compile tools/autobuild/auto_revert.py tools/autobuild/test_auto_revert.py`; `bash -n tools/build_loop/build_orchestrator.sh`; `python3 -m json.tool core/BUILD/REGISTRY.json >/tmp/d077-registry.json`; `git diff --check`
- **Independent review:** completed by subagent. Initial review found blockers in orchestrator worktree targeting, failed-head preservation, and post-reset verification; fixes landed. Second review found report-ID collision risk; fixed with microsecond/PID/UUID IDs and uniqueness regression. Final blocker-only re-review found no remaining P1/P2 blockers.
- **Risks:** `--allow-dirty` enables `git clean -fd` after `git reset --hard`; it is intended only for isolated generated build worktrees. The orchestrator passes `--repo-root "$wt"` to keep destructive cleanup out of the main checkout. No push, merge, deploy, secrets access, outreach send, money movement, timer install, or live service mutation was performed.
- **Rollback:** revert `tools/autobuild/auto_revert.py`, `tools/autobuild/test_auto_revert.py`, and the d077 block in `tools/build_loop/build_orchestrator.sh`; remove `core/BUILD/results/SPEC_d077_auto-revert-on-break.result.md`, the d077 registry row, and this handoff entry. - **Files changed:** `tools/autobuild/doc_only_merge_resolver.py`; `tools/autobuild/test_doc_only_merge_resolver.py`; `core/BUILD/specs/SPEC_d068_auto-merge-resolver-for-doc-only.md`; `core/BUILD/results/SPEC_d068_auto-merge-resolver-for-doc-only.result.md`; `core/BUILD/REGISTRY.json`; `docs/codex/HANDOFF.md`
- **Summary:** Added a guarded doc-only auto-merge resolver in the non-protected autobuild lane. It scans `core/BUILD/REGISTRY.json` for `done` builds with `tests_passed=true`, dry-runs by default, and with `--execute` locally merges only doc-only branches into `feat/headless-build` after an immediate independent AI review command approves. It rejects `main`/`master`, requires a clean worktree, skips missing/already-merged branches, aborts failed merges, and never pushes, deploys, or opens PRs. The doc-only allowlist excludes code/runtime paths plus build intake surfaces (`core/BUILD/specs/`, `core/BUILD/intents/`).
- **Tests:** `python3 -B -m unittest tools.autobuild.test_doc_only_merge_resolver` (10 tests OK); `env PYTHONPYCACHEPREFIX=/tmp/fpai-pycache python3 -m py_compile tools/autobuild/doc_only_merge_resolver.py tools/autobuild/test_doc_only_merge_resolver.py`; `bash -n tools/merge/process_merge_requests.sh`; `python3 -m json.tool core/BUILD/REGISTRY.json`; `git diff --check`; `python3 tools/autobuild/doc_only_merge_resolver.py --json` real-registry dry run with no merge executed.
- **Independent review:** completed by subagent. Initial review found blockers: `--base main` was possible, failed merge conflicts could leave the repo conflicted, `tools/merge/` is protected, and `core/BUILD/specs/`/`core/BUILD/intents/` were too broad for doc-only. Fixed all four by moving to `tools/autobuild/`, reverting `process_merge_requests.sh`, rejecting protected bases, aborting failed merges, and narrowing the allowlist. Blocker-only re-review found no remaining P1/P2 blockers.
- **Risks:** This branch does not install or wire a live timer/service. Ember/server must explicitly decide where to invoke `tools/autobuild/doc_only_merge_resolver.py --execute`. If the configured independent review command cannot run, the resolver blocks rather than merges. Build specs/intents remain intentionally excluded from doc-only auto-merge.
- **Rollback:** revert the two `tools/autobuild/` files and remove the d068 result/registry/handoff entries. If Ember wires invocation later, disable that invocation or remove `--execute`.
- **Intent solved:** AI-approved doc-only branches can be resolved by a guarded repo tool instead of requiring James to run shell merge commands.
- **Downstream intent unlocked:** Ember/server can wire low-risk documentation completion merges into the build loop while code/runtime branches stay on the normal review/merge path.
- **Questions for Ember/James:** choose the invocation point for `python3 tools/autobuild/doc_only_merge_resolver.py --execute` after this branch is merged; keep it disabled for branches that touch code, specs/intents, services, cron/systemd, secrets, or any non-doc path.

## How this works (the loop)
1. **Codex** reads `AGENTS.md` → `docs/codex/README.md` → **this file** → `docs/codex/ATTENTION_FLOW.md` → the spec. Builds on its branch. Posts results in 📥.
2. **Ember** keeps 📍 current + the 📤 queue; reads 📥; logs finished work to the vault `PROOF LOG` + `AGENT RUN LEDGER`; writes a BRICK if reusable; **mirrors this file to `00_MEMORY/CODEX HANDOFF.md` in the vault** so James sees it.
3. **James** reads the vault `[[CODEX HANDOFF]]` (Obsidian) for the same picture; approves diffs (desktop/phone).

*Single source of truth for "where are we": this board. Journals (`JAMES JOURNAL` / `EMBER JOURNAL`) are reflections; PROOF LOG is shipped record; this is the live coordination.*
